THE IDOLS BAN WITH THE SANCTUARY CARVE-OUT [claim LV26-01]. 'Make no אֱלִילִים (elilim, idols)' — one of the contemptuous names for idolatry; graven image and pillar each disqualified when made and each under 'you shall not set up' — the Mercury- heaps on the roads (Rabban Shimon b. Gamliel: the sun-images on the roofs); 'a figured stone... IN YOUR LAND to bow upon' — in your land on no stone, but on the SANCTUARY'S stones you do bow: the place clause carves the sanctuary out. Onkelos: טַעֲוָן ('errors') for idols — the contempt performed; אֶבֶן סָגְדָא ('a stone of bowing') — defined by use. 		—Sifra, Behar, Chapter 9 5, Onkelos Lev 26:1witnessed
Top split L mark=etnachta (major mid-verse rest); maps_to this verse only.

THE SOLD MAN'S CHARTER [claim LV26-02]. 'My sabbaths keep, My sanctuary revere' — addressed to THE ONE SOLD TO A GENTILE: let him not say 'my master worships idols, I too; uncovers nakedness, I too; profanes the Sabbath, I too' — the chapter's last two verses warn the sold man on every commandment; 'I am the LORD' — faithful to pay reward. Onkelos: לְבֵית מַקְדְּשִׁי תְּהוֹן דָּחֲלִין ('to the house of My sanctuary you shall be in awe'). 		—Sifra, Behar, Chapter 9 6, Onkelos Lev 26:2witnessed

WITNESS_READWITNESS-READ(im_bechukotai_telechu, the_two_clause_split_and_the_remember_verb_rule)
= WITNESS-READ(if-bechukotai-telechu, the-two-clause-split-and-the-remember-verb-rule)
THE TWO-CLAUSE SPLIT AND THE REMEMBER-VERB RULE [claim LV26-03]. 'Walk in My statutes' cannot be the commandments — 'keep My commandments and do them' says that — so walking = LABOR IN TORAH (the Omnipresent desires it: Psalm 81, Isaiah 48, Deut 5); the curse's 'not listen to Me' splits the same way at 26:14. THE REMEMBER-VERB RULE: 'remember the Sabbath' — 'keep' covers the heart, so remember = RECITE WITH THE MOUTH; the same pairing at the desert provocation, Miriam, Amalek — four seats. 'Keep and DO' — one who learns in order to do; who learns not to do, better uncreated. Onkelos: בִּקְיָמַי תְּהָכוּן ('in My statutes you shall walk') — the walking kept, the labor not written in. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Section 1 1, Sifra, Bechukotai, Section 1 2, Sifra, Bechukotai, Section 1 3, Sifra, Bechukotai, Section 1 5, Onkelos Lev 26:3witnessed

THE RAINS' SCHEDULE SCOPED TO THE LAND [claim LV26-04]. 'Rains in their season' — Wednesday nights (Sabbath-eve rain a curse sign even in Elijah's years); Herod's night rains dried by morning; Shimon b. Shetach's and Queen Salome's Sabbath-night rains growing wheat like kidneys, barley like olive stones, lentils like gold dinars — samples stored 'to show how much sin costs' (Jeremiah 5:25). 'YOUR rains' — not all lands': plenty here and famine there, the nations buying (Joseph's silver; Deut 33:25). Onkelos: מַטְרֵיכוֹן בְּעִדָּנְהוֹן ('your rains in their seasons'). 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 1 1, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 1 2, Onkelos Lev 26:4witnessed

THE SEASONS CHAINED AND THE LAND CLAUSE [claim LV26-05]. 'Threshing reaches vintage, vintage reaches sowing' — busy at each until the next; 'eat to satiety' — a little, blessed in the belly (Exod 23:25); 'dwell securely IN YOUR LAND' — not outside it. Onkelos: דְּיָשָׁא יָת קְטָפָא ('threshing the vintage'). 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 1 7, Onkelos Lev 26:5witnessed

PEACE WEIGHED AGAINST ALL, THE BEAST THAT CEASES, THE ARMIES IN TRANSIT [claim LV26-06]. 'Peace in the land' — lest you say food and drink are nothing without peace: peace weighed against everything (Isaiah 45:7). 'Remove the evil beast' — R. Yehuda: removed from the world; R. Shimon: made to CEASE HARMING (harmers present and not harming is the praise) — and ONKELOS SITS ON R. SHIMON'S ARM: וֶאֱבַטֵּל חַיְתָא בִשְׁתָּא ('I will make the evil beast cease'). 'No sword passes' — no ARMIES IN TRANSIT between provinces (as in Josiah's days) — Onkelos: וּדְקָטְלִין בְּחַרְבָּא לָא יְעִדּוּן ('those who kill by the sword shall not pass through'): the Sifra's reading written into the verse. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 1 8, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 2 1, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 2 3, Onkelos Lev 26:6witnessed

THE NON-LINEAR SCALING [claim LV26-07]. 'Five of you chase a hundred, a hundred ten thousand' — 'of you,' the WEAK among you; and the ratio is not arithmetic (five to a hundred would give a hundred to two thousand): THE MANY WHO DO TORAH ARE NOT AS THE FEW — a superlinear law read off the verse's own numbers; 'fall by the sword' — not in the way of the world. Onkelos keeps the numbers exactly. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 2 4, Onkelos Lev 26:8witnessed

TURNING IN GOOD AND THE NEW COVENANT [claim LV26-08]. 'I will turn to you' — the king's many laborers and the one who worked long: 'a great reckoning I will make with you'; 'turn' — in good; 'fruitful' — offspring; 'multiply' — upright stature; 'establish My covenant' — NOT like the first which you broke (Jeremiah 31:31-32, the new covenant). Onkelos: וְאֶתְפְּנִי בְמֵימְרִי לְאוֹטָבָא לְכוֹן ('I will turn BY MY WORD to do you good') — the Sifra's 'in good' written in. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 2 5, Onkelos Lev 26:9witnessed

OLD OF OLD [claim LV26-09]. 'Old, old-stored' — the older the better; 'old-stored' alone would be wine — 'old, old-stored' includes everything that ages; 'clear the old before the new' — granaries full of new, storehouses full of old, and you anxious how to clear the old. Onkelos plain. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 3 1, Onkelos Lev 26:10witnessed

MY DWELLING IS THE TEMPLE, AND THE PRESENCE WALKS [claim LV26-10]. 'My dwelling among you' — THE TEMPLE; 'My soul shall not abhor you' — once I redeem you I do not reject you again; 'I walk among you' — the king strolling with his tenant, 'I am like you'; 'I will be your God' — My awe still on you; 'who brought you out' — who did Egypt's wonders will do these. Onkelos: מַשְׁכְּנִּי ('My dwelling'); וְלָא יְרַחֵק מֵימְרִי יָתְכוֹן ('My Word shall not put you far'); וְאַשְׁרֵי שְׁכִינְתִּי בֵּינֵיכוֹן ('I will make My Presence dwell among you') for the walking — anthropomorphism replaced by the Presence. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 3 2, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 3 3, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 3 4, Onkelos Lev 26:11, Onkelos Lev 26:12witnessed

THE SEVEN-STEP DESCENT [claim LV26-13]. Seven clauses read as seven successive states, each entailing the next: not learning → not doing ('not do') → despising others who do ('despise My statutes') → hating the Sages ('abhor My judgments') → preventing others ('so as not to do') → denying Sinai's commandments ('all My commandments') → denying the Root ('break My covenant'). A state machine of decline compiled from one verse's clause order. Onkelos: לְאַשְׁנָיוּתְכוֹן יָת קְיָמִי ('to CHANGE My covenant') — breaking as alteration. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Section 2 3, Onkelos Lev 26:15witnessed

'Terror' — the plagues visiting one upon another; the plague of death. Onkelos keeps the four. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 4 1, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 4 2, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 4 3, Onkelos Lev 26:16witnessed

THE METALS' PHYSICS — AND ONKELOS WRITES IT IN [claim LV26-17]. 'Heavens as iron, earth as brass' is HARDER than Deuteronomy's 'heavens as brass, earth as iron': brass sweats, iron does not — there the sky gives some dew and the earth keeps its fruit; here the sky gives none and the earth sweats and rots its fruit. The two curse texts ordered by severity from the metals' behavior. 'Strength spent in vain' — Rabbi: the vineyard; flax; the strength; the father who dowered and buried his daughter in the wedding week; the laborer whose full work blight strikes — his teeth set on edge; the tree that sets and drops. Onkelos: תַּקִּיפִין כְּפַרְזְלָא מִלַּאֲחָתָא מִטְרָא ('hard as iron FROM SENDING DOWN RAIN'), חֲסִינָא כִנְחָשָׁא מִלְּמֶעְבַּד פֵּירִין ('strong as brass FROM MAKING FRUIT') — the metals' EFFECTS written into the verse. 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 5 3, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 5 4, Onkelos Lev 26:19witnessed

THE SWORD, THE CORPSE LAW, AND THE STAFF OF BREAD [claim LV26-19]. CONFESSION AND THE HUMBLED HEART [claim LV26-26]. 'They shall confess' — toward REPENTANCE: as soon as they confess, I return and have mercy; 'I too walk with them in hostility' — casual for casual; 'bring them into the enemies' land' — a GOOD measure: My prophets return them under My wings, against their will (Ezekiel 20:32-33); 'or then their uncircumcised heart is humbled' — as soon as they humble it, mercy. Onkelos: וִיוַדּוּן ('confess'); בְּשִׁקְרְהוֹן ('in their falsehood') for trespass; לִבְּהוֹן טַפְשָׁא ('their FOOLISH heart') for uncircumcised; יִרְעוּן יָת חוֹבֵיהוֹן ('accept their sins'). 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 3, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 4, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 5, Onkelos Lev 26:40, Onkelos Lev 26:41witnessed

THE FATHERS BACKWARD AND THE COVENANT TO THE LAND [claim LV26-27]. Why Jacob, Isaac, Abraham BACKWARD? if Abraham's deeds do not suffice, Isaac's; if not, Jacob's — each suffices to hang the world on: a fallback chain read off the order. 'Remember' for Abraham and Jacob, not Isaac — his ashes seen heaped on the altar; 'also' for Abraham and Isaac, not Jacob — his bed was whole: presence and absence of tokens each reasoned. The particle אֶת (et) — the MATRIARCHS (Genesis 49:31); 'the land I will remember' — the covenant cut to the LAND. Onkelos keeps the reversed order; וְאַרְעָא אֲנָא דָכִיר ('and the land I remember'). 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 6, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 7, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 8, Onkelos Lev 26:42witnessed

THE FOUR ERAS AND ONE IN A HUNDRED [claim LV26-28]. 'The land forsaken, paid its sabbaths' — the sabbath-debt restated; 'because and because' — not head for head: ONE IN A HUNDRED exacted; 'judgments' — the laws; 'statutes' — the expositions. 'This' — the desert's season; 'also this' — Peor's; 'and also this' — the Amorite kings'; 'not rejected' — Vespasian's days; 'abhorred' — Greece's; 'to destroy them, to break My covenant' — Haman's; 'I am the LORD their God' — Gog's: four clauses to four eras; the Torah scroll the one gift left. THE COVENANT TO THE TRIBES from 'the covenant of the ancestors.' Onkelos: לְוָטִין חֳלַף בִּרְכָן ('CURSES IN PLACE OF BLESSINGS') for 'because and because'; קְיַם קַדְמָאֵי ('the covenant of the first ones'). 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 9, Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 10, Onkelos Lev 26:43, Onkelos Lev 26:44, Onkelos Lev 26:45witnessed

TWO TORAHS [claim LV26-29]. 'Statutes, judgments, and TORAHS' — statutes = the expositions, judgments = the laws, torahs = TWO TORAHS given to Israel, one written and one oral (R. Akiva: many torahs — the burnt offering's, the meal offering's, the guilt offering's, the peace offering's, the tent's); 'between Him and the children of Israel' — Moses the agent; 'on Mount Sinai by Moses' hand' — the Torah with its laws, details, and explanations through Moses from Sinai. The book's closing self- description: two channels, the data channel's seventh self- label. Onkelos: אוֹרָיָתָא ('the torahs') plural kept; בֵּין מֵימְרֵיהּ וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל ('between His Word and the children of Israel'). 		—Sifra, Bechukotai, Chapter 8 10, Onkelos Lev 26:46witnessed
